    COMPANY PROFILE
    公司概況



    Easou Technology Holdings Limited is an exempted company                  宜搜科技控股有限公司為一家於2022年2月9日在
    incorporated in the Cayman Islands with limited liability on February     開曼群島註冊成立的獲豁免有限公司，並於2024
    9, 2022, and was listed on the Main Board of the Stock Exchange on        年6月7日在聯交所主板上市。
    June 7, 2024.

    The Group was founded in 2005 and operates four business lines            本集團於2005年成立，並經營四條業務線，涵蓋
    covering online reading platform services, digital marketing services,    數字閱讀平台服務、數字營銷服務、網絡遊戲發行
    online games publishing services and other digital content services.      服務及其他數字內容服務。我們超過90.0%的收益
    We generated over 90.0% of our revenue from our advertising               產生自數字閱讀平台服務及數字營銷服務下提供的
    services provided under the online reading platform services and          廣告服務。
    digital marketing services.

    Our AI-based recommendation technology is the foundation that             我們的人工智能推薦技術為推動我們所有業務的基
    empowers all of our businesses. We are committed to continuously          礎。我們致力於持續改進人工智能推薦技術。我們
    improving the AI-based recommendation technology. We have                 已成功將包括六層的宜搜推薦引擎應用於四個應用
    successfully applied our Easou Recommendation Engine, which               場景（包括數字閱讀、數字營銷、網絡遊戲及其他
    consists of six layers, to four application scenarios, including online   數字內容），並計劃發掘於其他場景應用宜搜推薦
    reading, digital marketing, online games and other digital content,       引擎的商機。
    and plan to explore business opportunities to apply our Easou
    Recommendation Engine in additional scenarios.

    We have built an online platform around our Easou Recommendation          我們圍繞宜搜推薦引擎、用戶基礎及多元化數字內
    Engine, our user base and diversified digital content. Our Easou          容建立了一個網絡平台。宜搜推薦引擎驅動我們的
    Recommendation Engine drives our platform through which our               平台，我們的用戶及客戶可通過該平台享受涵蓋數
    users and customers can enjoy a wealth of digital content covering        字閱讀、廣告內容、網絡遊戲及音樂等豐富的數字
    online literature, advertising content, online games and music,           內容。我們的平台連繫跨場景數字內容，涉及如數
    among others. Our platform connects cross-scenario digital                字內容提供商、媒體渠道、用戶、廣告客戶及電信
    content, involves various participants, such as digital content           運營商等多個參與者，並可讓參與者之間互動。這
    providers, media channels, users, advertising customers and               可使我們能夠在不同業務線實現協同效應，並幫助
    telecommunications operators, and enables them to interact with           我們實現多元化收益變現。
    each other. This allows us to achieve synergy across our various
    business lines and helps us achieve diversified monetization.

RETIREMENT AND EMPLOYEE BENEFITS SCHEME                                     退休及僱員福利計劃

The employees of the Group’s subsidiaries which operate in China            本集團於中國營運的附屬公司的僱員須參加當地市
are required to participate in a central pension scheme operated by         政府實施的中央養老金計劃。該等附屬公司須就僱
the local municipal government. These subsidiaries are required to          員薪金按若干百分比向中央養老金計劃供款。該等
contribute a certain proportion of their payroll costs to the central       供款於根據中央養老金計劃的規則成為應付款項時
pension scheme. The contributions are charged to profit or loss as          自損益扣除。
they become payable in accordance with the rules of the central
pension scheme.

Details of the retirement and employee benefits schemes of the              有關本公司退休及僱員福利計劃的詳情載於綜合財
Company are set out in note 2.4 to the consolidated financial               務報表附註2.4。
statements.

     Equity Pledge Agreement                                                   股權質押協議

     Under the equity pledge agreement dated December 31, 2022                 根據外商獨資企業、宜搜深圳與宜搜深圳登記股東
     between the WFOE, Easou Shenzhen and the Registered                       訂立日期為2022年12月31日的股權質押協議（「股
     Shareholders of Easou Shenzhen (the “Equity Pledge Agreement”),           權質押協議」），宜搜深圳登記股東同意向外商獨資
     the Registered Shareholders of Easou Shenzhen agreed to pledge            企業質押(i)彼等各自在宜搜深圳的所有股份，包括

Loss Sharing                                                              虧損分擔

Under the relevant PRC laws and regulations, none of our Company          根據相關中國法律及法規，本公司或外商獨資企業
or the WFOE is legally required to share the losses of, or provide        均無法律規定須分擔綜合聯屬實體的虧損或向綜合
financial support to the Consolidated Affiliated Entities. Further, the   聯屬實體提供財務支援。此外，綜合聯屬實體為有
Consolidated Affiliated Entities are limited liability companies and      限責任公司，須對其本身的債務及虧損以及所擁有
shall be solely liable for its own debts and losses with assets and       的資產及財產承擔全部責任。
properties owned by them.

Despite the foregoing, given that our Group conducts certain of its       儘管有上文所述，鑒於本集團通過持有必要的中國
business operations in the PRC through the Consolidated Affiliated        經營許可證及批准的綜合聯屬實體在中國進行若干
Entities, which hold the requisite PRC operational licenses and           業務活動，並根據適用的會計原則將其財務狀況及
approvals, and that its financial position and results of operations      經營業績綜合至本集團財務報表中，倘綜合聯屬實
are consolidated into our Group’s financial statements under the          體蒙受損失，本公司業務、財務狀況及經營業績將
applicable accounting principles, our Company’s business, financial       受到不利影響。
position and results of operations would be adversely affected if the
Consolidated Affiliated Entities suffer losses.

Listing Rules Implications                                             上市規則涵義

The transactions contemplated under the Contractual Arrangements       合約安排項下擬進行的交易為本公司的持續關連交
are continuing connected transactions for our Company and are          易，因此須遵守上市規則第14A章項下申報、公告
subject to reporting, announcement and independent Shareholders’       及獨立股東批准之規定。董事（包括獨立非執行董
approval requirements under Chapter 14A of the Listing Rules. Our      事）認為(i)合約安排對本集團的法律架構及業務經
Directors, including our independent non-executive Directors, are      營至為重要；及(ii)合約安排乃按一般商業條款或
of the view that (i) the Contractual Arrangements are fundamental      有利於本集團的條款，在本集團的日常業務過程中
to our Group’s legal structure and business operations; and (ii) the   訂立，屬公平合理或對本集團有利，並符合股東的
Contractual Arrangements are on normal commercial terms or             整體利益。因此，儘管合約安排項下擬進行的交
on terms more favorable to our Group, in the ordinary and usual        易在技術上構成上市規則第14A章下的持續關連交
course of our Group’s business and are fair and reasonable or to the   易，但董事認為，鑒於本集團在合約安排項下關連
advantage of our Group and are in the interests of our Shareholders    交易規則方面處於特殊情況，就合約安排項下擬進
as a whole. Accordingly, notwithstanding that the transactions         行的所有交易而言，若嚴格遵守上市規則第14A章
contemplated under the Contractual Arrangements technically            所載規定，包括（其中包括）公告及獨立股東批准之
constitute continuing connected transactions under Chapter 14A         規定，將會令本公司承擔過重的負擔且不切實際，
of the Listing Rules, our Directors consider that, given that our      並會增加本公司不必要的行政成本。
Group is placed in a special situation in relation to the connected
transactions rules under the Contractual Arrangements, it would be
unduly burdensome and impracticable, and would add unnecessary
administrative costs to our Company, for all the transactions
contemplated under the Contractual Arrangements to be subject to
strict compliance with the requirements set out under Chapter 14A of
the Listing Rules, including, among other things, the announcement
and approval of independent Shareholders.

Chairman and Chief Executive Officer                                     主席及首席執行官

Pursuant to code provision C.2.1 of the CG Code, the roles of the        根據企業管治守則守則條文第C.2.1條，主席及首
chairman and the chief executive officer should be separate and          席執行官之角色應予區分及不應由同一人擔任。本
should not be performed by the same individual. The roles of             公司董事會主席及首席執行官的職務均由執行董事
the chairman of the Board and the chief executive officer of the         汪先生擔任。董事會相信，將本公司董事會主席及
Company are both performed by Mr. Wang, an executive Director.           首席執行官的職務均賦予同一人可使本公司在制定
The Board believes that vesting the roles of both chairman of the        業務戰略及執行業務計劃時獲得更高的響應、效率
Board and the chief executive officer of the Company in the same         及效力。此外，鑒於汪先生的豐富業內經驗及在本
individual enables the Company to achieve higher responsiveness,         集團過往發展中的重要作用，董事會認為，於上市
efficiency and effectiveness when formulating business strategies        日期後汪先生繼續擔任董事會主席對本集團的業務
and executing business plans. Furthermore, in view of Mr. Wang’s         前景有利，且由執行董事及獨立非執行董事組成的
extensive industrial experience and significant role in the historical   董事會之運作足以維持權力及權限之平衡。
development of our Group, the Board believes that it is beneficial
to the business prospects of our Group that Mr. Wang continues
to act as the chairman of the Board following the Listing Date, and
that the balance of power and authority is sufficiently maintained by
the operation of the Board, comprising the executive Directors and
independent non-executive Directors.

governance practices on a regular basis to ensure compliance with        確保遵守企業管治守則。
the CG Code.

     employees’ securities transactions on terms no less exacting than              進行證券交易的行為守則，以供可能掌握本公司的
     the standard set out in the Model Code for the compliance by its               未公開內幕消息的相關僱員遵照規定買賣本公司證
     relevant employees who are likely to be in possession of unpublished           券。
     inside information of the Company in respect of their dealings in the
     Company’s securities.

of Directors and other significant financial and operational matters.    尋求獨立專業意見，費用由本公司承擔。彼等亦獲
Directors could have recourse to seek independent professional           鼓勵向本公司高級管理層進行獨立諮詢。
advice in performing their duties at the Company’s expense and are

The daily management, administration and operation of the Group          本集團的日常管理、行政及營運交予高級管理層負

RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROL                                     風險管理及內部控制

The Board acknowledges that it is the responsibility of the Board for    董事會確認，董事會有責任維持足夠的內部控制系
maintaining an adequate internal control system to safeguard the         統以保障股東投資及本公司資產，並每年檢討系統
Shareholders’ investments and the Company’s assets and reviewing         之成效。本集團風險管理及內部控制系統有明確權
the effectiveness of such system on an annual basis. The Group’s         限的管理架構及完善的政策及程序，旨在促進業務
risk management and internal control systems are featured with           合法、合規及有效運營，確保財務報告的可靠性及
a defined management structure with limits of authority and well-        遵守適用的法律法規、識別及管理潛在風險及保障
rounded policies and procedures, and are designed to facilitate          本集團資產的安全。風險管理及內部控制系統旨在
legal, compliant and effective operation of business, to ensure          管理而非消除未能實現業務目標的風險，且僅可合
reliability of financial reporting and compliance with applicable        理而非絕對保證不會出現重大錯誤陳述或損失。
laws and regulations, to identify and manage potential risks, and to
safeguard assets of the Group. The risk management and internal
control systems are designed to manage rather than eliminate
the risk of failure to achieve business objectives, and can only
provide reasonable and not absolute assurance against material
misstatements or loss.

      Governance Structure and Process                                       管治架構及流程

      To ensure the effectiveness of sustainability management, the          為確保可持續發展管理的有效性，公司初步建立了
      Company has initially established an ESG governance structure. The     ESG管治架構。董事會作為最高決策機構，全面負
      Board, as the highest decision-making body, is fully responsible for   責制定和監督公司的可持續發展戰略，並對ESG相
      formulating and supervising the Company’s sustainability strategies,   關的風險管理和內部監控系統的有效性承擔整體責

Dismissal, recruitment and       We adhere to the principles of openness, fairness and impartiality and have established

                                 have received adequate salary, severance pay or other compensation. If employees are

解僱、招聘及晉升                         我們堅持公開、公平、公正的原則，建立了完善的人才評估體系。在招聘過程中，我
                                 們綜合考量應聘者的專業能力、工作經驗、個人品格及發展潛力。在晉升方面，我們

                                 已按法例法規及本集團政策之要求進行離職手續，並已獲得足額之薪金、遣散費或其
                                 他補償。若員工遭解僱而離職，本集團會確保他們知悉相關原因。


Working hours and rest periods   We strictly abide by the working hours stipulated by the Labor Law and have
                                 implemented a flexible working system so that employees can better balance work and
                                 life. In addition to statutory holidays, we provide additional paid annual leave, marriage
                                 leave, maternity leave/paternity leave, compassionate leave, etc. Meanwhile, we also
                                 support our employees to apply to work from home under special circumstances,
                                 reflecting our humane management.
工作時數及假期                          我們嚴格遵守勞動法規定的工作時間，實行彈性工作制，讓員工能夠更好地平衡工作
                                 與生活。除法定節假日外，我們提供額外的帶薪年假、婚假、產假╱陪產假、恩恤假
                                 等。同時，我們也支持員工在特殊情況下申請在家辦公，體現人性化管理。


Equal opportunities              We provide equal development opportunities for all employees, regardless of gender,
                                 age, race, religious beliefs, marital status, disability, etc. In every aspect such as
                                 promotion, training and remuneration adjustment, we insist on using ability and
                                 performance as the only criteria for judgment to ensure that every employee receives
                                 fair treatment and development opportunities.
平等機會                             我們為所有員工提供平等的發展機會，不因性別、年齡、種族、宗教信仰、婚姻狀
                                 況、殘疾等因素而有所差異。在晉升、培訓、薪酬調整等各個環節，我們都堅持以能
                                 力和表現為唯一評判標準，確保每位員工都能獲得公平的對待和發展機會。

      Diversity policy                  We firmly believe that a diverse team can bring more innovative thinking and
                                        competitive advantages. As such, we actively recruit talents from different cultural
                                        backgrounds, educational backgrounds and professional fields, and create an open and
                                        inclusive corporate culture through various team-building activities, cultural exchange
                                        programs, etc., so as to facilitate communication and cooperation among employees
                                        from different backgrounds.
      多元化政策                             我們深信多元化的團隊能夠帶來更多創新思維和競爭優勢。因此，我們積極聘用來自
                                        不同文化背景、教育經歷和專業領域的人才，並通過多樣化的團建活動、文化交流計
                                        劃等方式，營造開放包容的企業文化氛圍，促進不同背景員工之間的交流與合作。


      Anti-discrimination measures      We have zero tolerance towards any form of discrimination, harassment or
                                        inappropriate behavior in the workplace. The Company has a dedicated anti-
                                        discrimination policy and complaint mechanism to ensure that employees can work in
                                        a safe and respectful environment. We regularly conduct relevant training to enhance
                                        employees’ anti-discrimination awareness, and have set up a special working group to
                                        handle relevant complaints and suggestions in a timely manner. All complaints shall

          discipline in business dealings and strictly prohibits any form       詳細列明了違規行為的界定及相應的處罰措

      Energy Consumption                                                         能源使用

      In terms of energy management, the Group’s main energy                     在能源管理層面，本集團的主要能源使用為電力，
      source is electricity. During the year, the Group actively adopted         故在年內積極採取了全面的節能措施來控制用電
      comprehensive energy saving measures to control electricity                量。這些措施包括充分利用自然光源以減少人工照
      consumption. These measures include making full use of natural             明需求、根據實際室溫情況靈活調節空調使用，以
      light to reduce the need for artificial lighting, flexibly adjusting the   及嚴格執行非辦公時間關閉電器設備的規定。通過
      use of air conditioning according to actual room temperature, and          這些措施的綜合實施，公司計劃在未來三年內每年
      strictly enforcing regulations on turning off electrical equipment         減少約5%的人均電力消耗。
      during non-office hours. Through the combined implementation
      of these measures, the Company plans to reduce electricity
      consumption per employee by approximately 5% every year over the
      next three years.

      Climate Change Management                                                氣候變化管理

      In response to climate change, the Company has adopted a                 在氣候變化應對方面，公司採取了積極的風險識別
      proactive risk identification and management strategy in the hope of     和管理策略，期望能將相關的負面影響降到最低。
      minimizing relevant negative impacts. In the preliminary review of       在初步的氣候風險的檢視中，我們可將相關風險分
      climate risks, we can divide the relevant risks into two categories:     為兩大類，一是實質風險，如極端天氣可能對公司
      one is physical risks, such as the impact that extreme weather may       設備設施和員工安全造成的影響；二是過渡風險，
      have on the Company’s equipment, facilities and employee safety;         主要涉及環境法規變化可能帶來的合規成本增加。
      the other is transition risks, which mainly involve the increase in      不過，基於目前的業務性質評估，公司認為氣候變
      compliance costs that may be caused by changes in environmental          化不會對其業務營運、財務表現及增長策略產生重
      regulations. However, based on the current assessment on the             大影響。
      nature of business, the Company believes that climate change will
      not have a significant impact on its business operations, financial
      performance and growth strategy.

           detecting a material misstatement resulting from fraud is               陳述，或淩駕於內部控制之上，因此未能發
           higher than for one resulting from error, as fraud may involve          現由於欺詐而導致之重大錯誤陳述之風險高
           collusion, forgery, intentional omissions, misrepresentations,          於未能發現由於錯誤而導致之重大錯誤陳述
           or the override of internal control.                                    之風險。

      •    Obtain an understanding of internal control relevant to the         •   了解與審計相關之內部控制，以設計適當之
           audit in order to design audit procedures that are appropriate          審計程序，但目的並非對貴集團內部控制之
           in the circumstances, but not for the purpose of expressing an          有效性發表意見。
           opinion on the effectiveness of the Group’s internal control.

      •    Evaluate the appropriateness of accounting policies used            •   評價董事所採用之會計政策的恰當性及所作
           and the reasonableness of accounting estimates and related              出之會計估計和相關披露的合理性。
           disclosures made by the directors.

      •    Conclude on the appropriateness of the directors’ use of the        •   對董事採用持續經營為會計基礎的恰當性作
           going concern basis of accounting and, based on the audit               出結論。根據所獲取之審計憑證，確定是否
           evidence obtained, whether a material uncertainty exists                存在與事項或情況有關之重大不確定因素，
           related to events or conditions that may cast significant doubt         從而可能導致對貴集團之持續經營能力產生
           on the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern. If we            重大疑慮。倘吾等認為存在重大不確定因
           conclude that a material uncertainty exists, we are required to         素，則須在核數師報告中提請注意綜合財務
           draw attention in our auditor’s report to the related disclosures       報表中之相關披露，或倘有關披露不足，則
           in the consolidated financial statements or, if such disclosures        須修訂意見。吾等之結論是基於核數師報告
           are inadequate, to modify our opinion. Our conclusions are              日期止所取得之審計憑證。然而，未來事項
           based on the audit evidence obtained up to the date of our              或情況可能導致貴集團不能繼續持續經營。
           auditor’s report. However, future events or conditions may
           cause the Group to cease to continue as a going concern.

               Basis of consolidation                                                   綜合基準

               The consolidated financial statements include the                        綜合財務報表包括本公司及其附屬公司
               financial statements of the Company and its subsidiaries                 截至2024年12月31日止年度的財務報
               for the year ended 31 December 2024. A subsidiary is an                  表。附屬公司是指本公司直接或間接控
               entity (including a structured entity), directly or indirectly,          制的實體（包括結構性實體）。當本集團
               controlled by the Company. Control is achieved when the                  通過參與投資對象的相關活動而獲得或
               Group is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from                有權獲得可變回報，並且有能力運用對
               its involvement with the investee and has the ability to                 投資對象的權力（即賦予本集團現有能
               affect those returns through its power over the investee                 力以主導投資對象的相關活動的現有權
               (i.e., existing rights that give the Group the current ability           利）影響該等回報時，即取得控制權。
               to direct the relevant activities of the investee).

               Generally, there is a presumption that a majority of                     一般情況下均存在多數投票權形成控制
               voting rights results in control. When the Company has                   權的推定。倘本公司擁有少於投資對象
               less than a majority of the voting or similar rights of                  大多數投票或類似權利，則本集團於評
               an investee, the Group considers all relevant facts and                  估其是否擁有對投資對象的權力時會考
               circumstances in assessing whether it has power over an                  慮一切相關事實及情況，包括：
               investee, including:

               (a)   the contractual arrangement with the other vote                    (a)   與投資對象的其他投票權持有人
                     holders of the investee;                                                 的合約安排；

               (b)   rights arising from other contractual                              (b)   其他合約安排所產生的權利；及
                     arrangements; and

               (c)   the Group’s voting rights and potential voting                     (c)   本集團的投票權及潛在投票權。
                     rights.

               The financial statements of the subsidiaries are                         附屬公司採用與本公司一致的會計政策
               prepared for the same reporting period as the Company,                   編製同一相關期間的財務報表。附屬公
               using consistent accounting policies. The results of                     司的業績由本集團獲得控制權之日起予
               subsidiaries are consolidated from the date on which the                 以綜合，並繼續綜合直至該控制權停止
               Group obtains control, and continue to be consolidated                   當日止。
               until the date that such control ceases.

           2.4 MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                      2.4 重要會計政策

               Investments in associates                                           於聯營公司之投資

               An associate is an entity in which the Group has a long             聯營公司為本集團長期擁有一般不少於
               term interest of generally not less than 20% of the equity          20%股本投票權，並對其有重大影響力
               voting rights and over which it has significant influence,          之實體。重大影響力乃有權參與投資對
               Significant influence is the power to participate in the            象之財務及經營政策決定而非控制或共
               financial and operating policy decisions of the investee,           同控制該等政策。
               but is not control or joint control over those policies.

               Business combinations and goodwill                                  業務合併及商譽

               Business combinations are accounted for using the                   業務合併按收購法列賬。所轉讓代價乃
               acquisition method. The consideration transferred is                按收購日期的公平值計量，而該公平值
               measured at the acquisition date fair value which is                為本集團轉讓的資產於收購日期的公平
               the sum of the acquisition date fair values of assets               值、本集團自被收購方的前擁有人承擔
               transferred by the Group, liabilities assumed by the                的負債及本集團發行以換取被收購方控
               Group to the former owners of the acquiree and the                  制權的股本權益的總和。就各項業務合
               equity interests issued by the Group in exchange for                併而言，本集團選擇以公平值或被收購
               control of the acquiree. For each business combination,             方可識別資產淨值的應佔比例，計量於
               the Group elects whether to measure the non-                        被收購方的非控股權益。非控股權益的
               controlling interests in the acquiree at fair value or at           所有其他部分乃按公平值計量。收購相
               the proportionate share of the acquiree’s identifiable net          關成本於產生時支銷。
               assets. All other components of non-controlling interests
               are measured at fair value. Acquisition-related costs are
               expensed as incurred.

               The Group determines that it has acquired a business                當所收購的一組活動及資產包含共同對
               when the acquired set of activities and assets includes             創造產出的能力有重大貢獻的一項投入
               an input and a substantive process that together                    及一項實質性程序，本集團認為其已收
               significantly contribute to the ability to create outputs.          購一項業務。

               When the Group acquires a business, it assesses the                 本集團收購一項業務時，會根據於收購
               financial assets and liabilities assumed for appropriate            日期的合約條款、經濟狀況及相關條件
               classification and designation in accordance with                   評估所收購的金融資產及所承擔的金融
               the contractual terms, economic circumstances and                   負債，以作出適當分類及指定，包括區
               pertinent conditions as at the acquisition date. This               分被收購方主合同中的嵌入式衍生品。
               includes the separation of embedded derivatives in host
               contracts of the acquiree.

         Fair value measurement                                                公平值計量

         The Group measures its unlisted fund investments at fair              本集團於各報告期末按公平值計量其非
         value at the end of each reporting period. Fair value is              上市基金投資。公平值指於計量日市場
         the price that would be received to sell an asset or paid             參與者之間的有序交易中，就出售資產
         to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between             所收取或轉讓負債所支付的價格。公平
         market participants at the measurement date. The fair                 值計量乃基於假設出售資產或轉讓負債
         value measurement is based on the presumption that                    的交易於該資產或負債的主要市場或
         the transaction to sell the asset or transfer the liability           （在未有主要市場的情況下）該資產或負
         takes place either in the principal market for the asset              債的最有利市場進行。主要或最有利市
         or liability, or in the absence of a principal market, in             場須為本集團能參與的市場。假設市場
         the most advantageous market for the asset or liability.              參與者基於最佳經濟利益行事的情況
         The principal or the most advantageous market must be                 下，資產或負債的公平值乃使用市場參
         accessible by the Group. The fair value of an asset or a              與者為資產或負債定價時所用的假設計
         liability is measured using the assumptions that market               量。
         participants would use when pricing the asset or liability,
         assuming that market participants act in their economic
         best interest.

         A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes               非金融資產的公平值計量會考慮市場參
         into account a market participant’s ability to generate               與者將資產用於最高增值及最佳用途或
         economic benefits by using the asset in its highest and               將資產出售予會將資產用於最高增值及
         best use or by selling it to another market participant that          最佳用途的另一名市場參與者而創造經
         would use the asset in its highest and best use.                      濟利益的能力。

         The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate              本集團針對不同情況使用恰當的估值技
         in the circumstances and for which sufficient data are                術，確保有足夠數據可用於計量公平
         available to measure fair value, maximising the use of                值，並儘量利用相關可觀察輸入數據，
         relevant observable inputs and minimising the use of                  儘量減少使用不可觀察輸入數據。
         unobservable inputs.

         amount is estimated. An asset’s recoverable amount is              產的可收回金額指資產或現金產生單位
         the higher of the asset’s or cash-generating unit’s value          的使用價值與其公平值減出售成本兩者
         in use and its fair value less costs of disposal, and is           之中的較高者，且按個別資產釐定，除
         determined for an individual asset, unless the asset does          非該資產所產生的現金流入並非基本上
         not generate cash inflows that are largely independent             獨立於其他資產或資產組別，在該情況
         of those from other assets or groups of assets, in which           下可收回金額則按資產所屬現金產生單
         case the recoverable amount is determined for the cash-            位釐定。
         generating unit to which the asset belongs.

         In testing a cash-generating unit for impairment, a                在對現金產生單位進行減值測試時，倘
         portion of the carrying amount of a corporate asset is             能按合理一致基準分配，則部分公司資
         allocated to an individual cash-generating unit if it can          產的賬面值分配至個別現金產生單位，
         be allocated on a reasonable and consistent basis or,              否則將分配至最小現金產生單位組別。
         otherwise, to the smallest group of cash-generating
         units.

         An impairment loss is recognised only if the carrying              僅當資產的賬面值超逾其可收回金額時
         amount of an asset exceeds its recoverable amount. In              方會確認減值虧損。於評估使用價值
         assessing value in use, the estimated future cash flows            時，估計未來現金流量按可反映貨幣時
         are discounted to their present value using a pre-tax              間價值及資產特有風險的當前市場評估
         discount rate that reflects current market assessments             的稅前貼現率貼現至其現值。減值虧損
         of the time value of money and the risks specific to the           於產生期間在損益中列入與減值資產功
         asset. An impairment loss is charged to profit or loss in          能屬於同一類別的開支。
         the period in which it arises in those expense categories
         consistent with the function of the impaired asset.

               In order for a financial asset to be classified and                 為使金融資產按攤銷成本分類及計量，
               measured at amortised cost, it needs to give rise to cash           需要就未償還本金產生純粹本息付款
               flows that are solely payments of principal and interest            （「純粹本息付款」）的現金流量。就現金
               (“SPPI”) on the principal amount outstanding. Financial             流量並非純粹本息付款之金融資產而
               assets with cash flows that are not SPPI are classified             言，不論業務模式如何，均按公平值計
               and measured at fair value through profit or loss,                  入損益分類及計量。
               irrespective of the business model.

               The Group’s business model for managing financial                   本集團管理金融資產的業務模式指其如
               assets refers to how it manages its financial assets                何管理其金融資產以產生現金流量。業
               in order to generate cash flows. The business model                 務模式確定現金流量是否來自收集合約
               determines whether cash flows will result from                      現金流量、出售金融資產，或兩者兼
               collecting contractual cash flows, selling the financial            有。於旨在持有金融資產以收取合約現
               assets, or both. Financial assets classified and measured           金流量的業務模式中持有的金融資產按
               at amortised cost are held within a business model with             攤銷成本分類及計量。並無於上述業務
               the objective to hold financial assets in order to collect          模式中持有的金融資產按公平值計入損
               contractual cash flows. Financial assets which are not              益分類及計量。
               held within the aforementioned business models are
               classified and measured at fair value through profit or
               loss.

               Purchases or sales of financial assets that require                 一般須在由法規或市場慣例確定的期間
               delivery of assets within the period generally established          內移交的金融資產購買及出售均於交易
               by regulation or convention in the marketplace are                  日（即本集團承諾購買或出售資產之日）
               recognised on the trade date, that is, the date that the            確認。
               Group commits to purchase or sell the asset.

         depends on their classification as follows:                           定：

         subject to impairment. Gains and losses are recognised                終止確認、修訂或減值時，收益及虧損
         in profit or loss when the asset is derecognised, modified            於損益內確認。
         or impaired.

         carried in the statement of financial position at fair value          值於財務狀況表列賬，並將公平值的變
         with net changes in fair value recognised in profit or loss.          動淨額於損益確認。

         This category includes unlisted fund investments which                該類別包括非上市基金投資，本集團並
         the Group had not irrevocably elected to classify at fair             無不可撤回地選擇通過其他全面收益按
         value through other comprehensive income.                             公平值分類。

         Impairment of financial assets                                          金融資產減值

         The Group recognises an allowance for expected credit                   本集團確認對並非按公平值計入損益計
         losses (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not held at                    量持有的所有債務工具預期信貸虧損
         fair value through profit or loss. ECLs are based on                    （「預期信貸虧損」）的撥備。預期信貸虧
         the difference between the contractual cash flows due                   損乃基於根據合約到期的合約現金流量
         in accordance with the contract and all the cash flows                  與本集團預期收取的所有現金流量之間
         that the Group expects to receive, discounted at an                     的差額而釐定，並以原實際利率的概約
         approximation of the original effective interest rate. The              值貼現。預期現金流量將包括出售所持
         expected cash flows will include cash flows from the sale               抵押的現金流量或組成合約條款的其他
         of collateral held or other credit enhancements that are                信貸提升措施。
         integral to the contractual terms.

         General approach                                                        一般方法

         ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit exposures                 預期信貸虧損按兩個階段確認。就信貸
         for which there has not been a significant increase in                  風險自初步確認起並無顯著增加的信用
         credit risk since initial recognition, ECLs are provided                敞口而言，根據因未來12個月內可能
         for credit losses that result from default events that are              發生的違約事件導致的信貸虧損(12個
         possible within the next 12 months (a 12-month ECL).                    月預期信貸虧損）計提預期信貸虧損撥
         For those credit exposures for which there has been a                   備。就信貸風險自初步確認起已有顯著
         significant increase in credit risk since initial recognition,          增加的有關信貸風險而言，則須就該敞
         a loss allowance is required for credit losses expected                 口的餘下年期預期信貸虧損計提虧損撥
         over the remaining life of the exposure, irrespective of the            備，而不論違約事件發生的時間（全期
         timing of the default (a lifetime ECL).                                 預期信貸虧損）。

               date with the risk of a default occurring on the financial            同時考慮了毋須使用過多成本或努力即
               instrument as at the date of initial recognition and                  可獲得的合理的和可支持的資料，包括
               considers reasonable and supportable information that is              歷史和前瞻性資料。本集團認為，當合
               available without undue cost or effort, including historical          約付款逾期超過30日時，信貸風險將
               and forward-looking information. The Group considers                  大幅增加。
               that there has been a significant increase in credit risk
               when contractual payments are more than 30 days past
               due.

               The Group considers a financial asset in default when                 本集團將合約付款逾期90日的金融資
               contractual payments are 90 days past due. However,                   產視作違約。然而，於若干情況下，在
               in certain cases, the Group may also consider a                       計及本集團作出的任何信貸提升措施
               financial asset to be in default when internal or external            前，本集團亦可於內部或外部資料顯示

               the outstanding contractual amounts in full before                    金額時將金融資產視作違約。金融資產
               taking into account any credit enhancements held by                   於無法合理預期可收回合約現金流量時
               the Group. A financial asset is written off when there is             撇銷。
               no reasonable expectation of recovering the contractual
               cash flows.

               For the purpose of the consolidated statement of cash                  就綜合現金流量表而言，現金及現金等

               and at banks, and short-term deposits as defined above,                短期存款，再扣除須於要求時償還且為
               less bank overdrafts which are repayable on demand and                 本集團現金管理組成部分的銀行透支。
               form an integral part of the Group’s cash management.

               Income tax                                                             所得稅

               Income tax comprises current and deferred tax. Income                  所得稅包括即期及遞延稅項。與於損益
               tax relating to items recognised outside profit or loss                以外確認項目相關的所得稅於損益以外
               is recognised outside profit or loss, either in other                  的其他全面收益或直接於權益確認。
               comprehensive income or directly in equity.

    a credit control department to minimise credit risk. Overdue         貸風險降至最低。逾期結餘由高級管理層定

    view of the aforementioned and the fact that the Group’s trade       項涉及大量多元化客戶，故並無重大集中信

    there is no significant concentration of credit risk. The Group      持有任何抵押品或其他信貸提升措施。貿易

          An impairment analysis is performed at each reporting date           於各報告日期使用撥備矩陣進行減值分析，
          using a provision matrix to measure expected credit losses.          以計量預期信貸虧損。撥備率基於具有類似
          The provision rates are based on days past due for groupings of      虧損模式的不同客戶分部分組的預期天數（即
          various customer segments with similar loss patterns (i.e., by       按地理區域、產品類型、客戶類型及評級）。
          geographical region, product type, customer type and rating).        該計算反映概率加權結果、貨幣的時間價值
          The calculation reflects the probability-weighted outcome,           及於報告日期可獲得有關過往事件、當前狀
          the time value of money and reasonable and supportable               況及未來經濟狀況預測的合理有據的資料。
          information that is available at the reporting date about past       一般而言，倘貿易應收款項逾期超過一年，
          events, current conditions and forecasts of future economic          則會予以撇銷，且不受強制執行活動所規限。
          conditions. Generally, trade receivables are written off if past
          due for more than one year and are not subject to enforcement
          activity.

                       the Group, which represents the consideration paid                     作出的分派指為推動本集團重
                       to the then shareholders of Easou Shenzhen in                          組支付予宜搜深圳當時股東的代
                       order to facilitate the reorganisation of the Group.                   價。

          (b)   Capital reserve                                                  (b)   資本儲備

                the shareholders resulted from transactions in prior                   作股東出資。
                years.

          (c)   Statutory surplus reserve                                        (c)   法定盈餘儲備

                In accordance with the PRC Company Law and the                         根據中國公司法及於中國成立的附屬公
                articles of association of the subsidiaries established in             司的組織章程細則，各相關附屬公司須
                the PRC, each of the relevant subsidiaries is required to              根據中國會計準則釐定按其除稅後純利
                appropriate 10% of its net profit after tax, as determined             的10%提取法定盈餘儲備，直至儲備餘
                under the Chinese Accounting Standards, to the statutory               額達到其註冊資本的50%。受相關中國
                surplus reserve until the reserve balance reaches 50% of               法規及相關附屬公司組織章程細則所載
                its registered capital. Subject to certain restrictions set            若干限制的規限，法定盈餘儲備可用於
                out in the relevant PRC regulations and in the articles                抵銷虧損或轉增股本，惟轉換後餘額不
                of association of the relevant subsidiaries, the statutory             得少於註冊資本的25%。儲備不得用作
                surplus reserve may be used either to offset losses, or                其設立目的以外的其他用途，亦不得作
                to be converted to increase the share capital provided                 為現金股息分派。
                that the balance after such conversion is not less than
                25% of registered capital. The reserve cannot be used for
                purposes other than those for which it is created and is
                not distributable as cash dividends.

          FINANCIAL INSTRUMENTS

          The carrying amounts of the Group’s financial instruments              本集團的金融工具的賬面值與其公平值合理
          reasonably approximate to their fair values.                           相若。

          Management has assessed that the fair values of trade                  管理層已評估貿易應收款項、計入預付款
          receivables, financial assets included in prepayments, deposits        項、按金及其他應收款項的金融資產、現金
          and other receivables, cash and cash equivalents, trade                及現金等價物、貿易應付款項、計入其他應
          payables, financial liabilities included in other payables and         付款項及應計費用的金融負債及計息銀行及
          accruals and the interest-bearing bank and other borrowings            其他借款之公平值，其數額與其賬面值相
          approximate to their carrying amounts largely due to the short         若，主要由於該等工具將於短期內到期。
          term maturities of these instruments.

          The Group’s corporate finance team headed by the finance               本集團金融工具公平值計量政策與程序由財
          manager is responsible for determining the policies and                務經理帶領的公司財務部負責釐定。公司
          procedures for the fair value measurement of financial                 財務部直接向首席財務官匯報。於各報告日
          instruments. The corporate finance team reports directly to            期，公司財務部對金融工具的價值變動進行
          the chief financial officer. At each reporting date, the corporate     分析，並決定估值採用的主要輸入數據。估
          finance team analyses the movements in the values of financial         值結果由首席財務官審閱核准。
          instruments and determines the major inputs applied in the
          valuation. The valuation is reviewed and approved by the chief
          financial officer.

          The fair values of the financial assets and liabilities are            金融資產及負債的公平值以自願交易方（強迫
          included at the amount at which the instrument could be                或清盤出售除外）當前交易中該工具的可交易
          exchanged in a current transaction between willing parties,            金額入賬。
          other than in a forced or liquidation sale.

          The Group’s principal financial instruments comprise cash and          本集團的主要金融工具包括現金及現金等價

          included in prepayments, deposits and other receivables, trade         其他應收款項的金融資產、貿易應付款項及

          are credit risk and liquidity risk. The directors review and           風險及流動資金風險。董事檢討並同意管理

          summarised below.

          parties. It is the Group’s policy that all customers who wish          交易。本集團的政策規定，所有擬按信貸期
          to trade on credit terms are subject to credit verification            進行交易的客戶，均須接受信貸核實程序。
          procedures. In addition, receivable balances are monitored on          此外，本集團持續監察應收款項結餘，面對
          an ongoing basis and the Group’s exposure to bad debts is not          的壞賬風險並不重大。
          significant.
